- **research-review**: Synthesise existing research — reports, prior studies, usage data — before the sprint starts, so the team builds on what's already known. Use before the sprint starts. Use when the team has conducted prior user research, usability studies, or surveys relevant to the sprint topic, or when the Decider or stakeholders have specific claims about user behaviour that need to be validated or surfaced. ([veevia/design-agency](https://github.com/veevia/design-agency/tree/HEAD/03_Agents-and-Skills/Skills/research-review) · ★1)
- **research-review**: Get a deep critical review of research via external reviewer. Use when user says "review my research", "help me review", "get external review", or wants critical feedback on research ideas, papers, or experimental results. ([yhsun060421/math-model-skills](https://github.com/yhsun060421/math-model-skills/tree/HEAD/02-%E7%A7%91%E7%A0%94%E6%B5%81%E7%A8%8B/research-review) · ★1 · 5 copies)
- **research-review**: Hostile peer review of a paper, from a research suite or a supplied PDF or text: scores novelty, rigor, reproducibility, ethics; ranks revisions. Use for 'review this paper like Reviewer 2', 'referee this PDF'. Rebuttal: research-disseminate; rewrite: research-paper; references: citation-auditor. ([Ahmed-G-Gad/apothem](https://github.com/Ahmed-G-Gad/apothem/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/behavior-diff/install_driver/rendered/hermes/.apothem/support/skills/research-review) · 6 copies)
- **research-review**: Run a tough but constructive internal critique of a research artifact: paper, draft, analysis, or simulation study. Use when the user asks for a review, critique, or feedback on a paper or draft, or wants weaknesses identified before submission. ([cameronbracken/waterology-research](https://github.com/cameronbracken/waterology-research/tree/HEAD/skills/research-review))
- **research-review**: Audit a market-microstructure result, report, or proposed claim for causal validity, weighting bias, uncertainty, and reproducibility. ([chamsrut/crypto-arb](https://github.com/chamsrut/crypto-arb/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/research-review))
- **research-review**: Thoroughly review research documents for accuracy, completeness, and actionability using the Rule of 5 iterative refinement process. ([charly-vibes/incitaciones](https://github.com/charly-vibes/incitaciones/tree/HEAD/content/distilled/research-review))
- **research-review**: Iterative review of research documents using Rule of 5. Use only when iteratively reviewing research documents. ([charly-vibes/incitaciones](https://github.com/charly-vibes/incitaciones/tree/HEAD/skills/research-review))
